###############################
# Mainline rite types         #
###############################
# One rite per faith that would otherwise only ever have an engine-generated
# dynamic rite. Dynamic rites get positional tags (dynamic_rite_%i) that script,
# history and localisation cannot reference, so every faith gets a stable
# scripted main rite instead.
# Keys, and therefore names, adjectives and adherent locs, match the parent
# faith so the player-facing text is unchanged.
# color, tenets, doctrines and tenet_selection_pair are copied from the faith
# type: an empty tenets block on a rite falls back to the religion's core
# tenets rather than the faith's, which would silently drop faith tenets.
#{

akom_pagan = {
	faith = akom_pagan

	color = { 0.9 0.7 0.3 }

	tenets = {
		tenet_adorcism
		tenet_ritual_celebrations
		tenet_ancestor_worship
	}

	doctrines = {
		west_african_unreformed_faith_doctrine
	}
}

baltic_pagan = { # Perkūnas — the religion of the Lithuanians, Samogitians and Curonians: Perkūnas the thunder and Dievas the sky, Žemyna the earth, Laima who allots each fate, Gabija of the hearth; the alka groves and hills; the sacred fire kept at Vilnius; the dead burned with their horses and arms. Main rite
	name = perkunas
	desc = perkunas_desc
	faith = baltic_pagan

	color = { 0.6 0.4 0.3 }

	tenets = {
		tenet_sacred_childbirth # Laima, who stands by every birth and allots the child its fate

bimoism = { # Bimoism — the religion of the Yi (Nuosu) of Liangshan, the Wumeng hills and the Dian lakes, heirs of the Wuman of Nanzhao: Ngeti Gunzy, god of the sky; Zhyge Alu, who shot down the suns; the bimo, hereditary priests who read and curse from books in the Yi script and send the souls of the dead on to the ancestors; the suni, drummers possessed by the spirits; the dead burned on the pyre. Main rite
	name = bimo
	desc = bimo_desc
	faith = bimoism

	color = { 154 123 93 }
	icon = bimoism

	tenets = {
		tenet_esotericism # The bimo's books and curses, learned only within the bimo lineages
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The ancestors' souls, kept in the maddu and sent on to the land of the ancestors by the bimo
		tenet_adorcism # The suni, drumming in trance as the spirits possess them
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines # Lords kept secondary wives; marriage stayed within one's own caste
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Cross-cousin marriage was preferred; marriage within the clan was forbidden
		doctrine_clerical_function_recruitment # The bimo cursed the enemy and read the omens before a raid
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # Bimo were men, the office passing from father to son
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime # The clan avenges its own; feuds ran for generations
		doctrine_deviancy_crime
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Families suspected of sorcery were shunned, and their daughters could not marry out
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_cremation # The dead were burned, as the Wuman of Nanzhao had burned theirs
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_mythic_literary # The bimo scriptures, written in the Yi script
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Liquor drunk from lacquered cups at every gathering
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

	localization = {

		CreatorName = yi_bimo_creator_god_name
		CreatorNamePossessive = yi_bimo_creator_god_name_possessive
		CreatorSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		CreatorHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		CreatorHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		HealthGodName = yi_bimo_health_god_name

nuo = { # Nuo — the religion of the Tujia of the Wuling mountains, heirs of the Ba and the Wuxi Man: Lin Jun, the first lord of the Ba, who became a white tiger at his death; the Eight Great Kings and the Peng lords of Xizhou, honoured in the baishou halls with the hand-waving dance; Nuogong and Nuomu, the brother and sister who survived the flood; the tima and the masked Nuo exorcists. Main rite
	faith = nuo

	color = { 20 33 71 }
	icon = nuo

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The Eight Great Kings and the ancestors of the Peng, honoured in the baishou halls
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # The baishou dance of the New Year

		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned

		doctrine_funeral_stoic # The dead are sent off with the sayeerh, danced all night around the coffin

dayawism = { # Dayawism — the Visayan religion of the diwata and the umalagad ancestor spirits; Kaptan and Magwayen, Laon of Kanlaon, Sidapa on Madya-as; the babaylan and asog mediums. Beliefs recorded by Miguel de Loarca (1582) and Francisco Alcina (1668). Main rite
	name = dayaw
	desc = dayaw_desc
	faith = dayawism

	color = { 125 229 84 }
	icon = dayawism

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_mountain_worship
		tenet_adorcism
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Daughters inherited equally with sons; women kept their own property and bride-price (Plasencia, Loarca)
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal # The datu rules; the babaylan serves
		doctrine_concubines # One principal wife; datus kept secondary wives and slave women
		doctrine_divorce_allowed # Divorce by return of the bride-price
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_homosexuality_accepted # The asog, men who lived as women and served as babaylan
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned # Settled by fines
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Sorcerers (mambabarang, aswang) were feared and killed
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The babaylan healed the sick and kept the rites of the dead
		doctrine_clerical_gender_female_only # Babaylan were women, or asog who lived as women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # Called by the diwata through illness or dream
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Kin wailed over the dead and kept the mourning taboos (Alcina)
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # Diwata (Sanskrit devata) and Bathala (bhattara): Indic loanwords for the gods themselves
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Tuba palm wine, basi and the pangasi rice beer of the feasts
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

javanist = { # Kejawèn — the hyang and ancestor worship of the Javanese countryside beneath the Hindu-Buddhist courts: the danyang village spirits and punden terraces, the sacred mountains, the petirtaan bathing places, and the hermits (wiku, ajar) of the mountain mandalas described in the Tantu Panggelaran. Main rite
	name = hyang
	desc = hyang_desc
	faith = javanist

		tenet_ancestor_worship # The danyang, founding spirits of each village, and the punden terraces of the ancestors
		tenet_mountain_worship # Dieng ("Di Hyang"), Lawu, Merapi, and Mahameru, which the gods carried from India to steady Java (Tantu Panggelaran)

	doctrines = {

aslian_animism = { # Cenoi — the religion of the Semang, the forest foragers of the Malay hills (Batek, Jahai, Kensiu, Kintaq, Lanoh, Mendriq): Karei of the thunderstorm, who punishes the breaking of taboo, and the blood offering thrown to the sky to calm him; Ta' Pedn the creator; the hala', shamans who sing with the cenoi, spirits of the flowers; the dead going west to the Isle of Fruit. Heirs of the Hoabinhian foragers; recorded by Skeat and Blagden (1906) and Paul Schebesta (1920s). Main rite
	name = cenoi
	desc = cenoi_desc
	faith = aslian_animism

	color = { 99 70 39 }
	icon = kadaic

	tenets = {
		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # The forest and its taboos: to mock an animal brings Karei's storm

		tenet_communal_possessions # Food is shared out through the whole camp; to keep it back is a wrong against Karei

dab_qhuas = { # Kev Dab Qhuas — the religion of the Hmong (Miao) of the Guizhou, western Hunan and southern Sichuan hills: the ancestors of each clan and the household spirits of the xwm kab altar; Siv Yis, the first shaman, and the txiv neeb who follow him into the spirit world to bring back the lost soul; the qeej and the Qhuab Ke, which show the dead the road back to the ancestors; Ntxwj Nyug, lord of the land of the dead. Main rite
	faith = dab_qhuas

	color = { 115 79 150 }
	icon = dab_qhuas

		tenet_reincarnation # The soul returns to the ancestors and is born again
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated # Patrilineal clans; a bride joins her husband's clan and his ancestors
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_polygamy # A man of standing kept several wives, and took his dead brother's widow as his own
		doctrine_divorce_approval # Settled by the elders of both clans, with the bride-price returned
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Marriage within the clan was forbidden; a cousin of another clan was the preferred match
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The txiv neeb heal by bringing back the lost soul
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # Txiv neeb are men and women alike

kaharingan = { # Hatalla — the religion of the Ngaju, Ot Danum and Ma'anyan of the Barito and Kahayan rivers, and of the Kayan, Punan and Murut of the interior: Ranying Hatalla Langit above and Jata Balawang Bulau below; the tiwah, the great feast of secondary burial, the sandung ossuaries and the Batang Garing, the tree of life; the basir and balian who chant in the language of the sangiang. Main rite
	name = hatalla
	desc = hatalla_desc
	faith = kaharingan

	color = { 86 239 245 }
	icon = kaharingan

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The tiwah: the bones of the dead are raised and laid in the sandung so the soul may reach Lewu Tatau
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # The tiwah feast, with its sapundu posts and buffalo sacrifice
	}

		requires_dlc_flag = all_under_heaven

		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # The basir and the balian, the manang and the dayung
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed # The basir, balian, manang and bobohizan all marry

		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned

kiratism = { # Mundhum — the religion of the Yakthung (Limbu): Tagera Ningwaphuma, the formless supreme; Yuma Sammang and Theba Sammang; the Mundhum, the sacred oral lore recited by the phedangma and samba; the yeba and yema who heal and drive out the sogha. Main rite
	name = mundhum
	desc = mundhum_desc
	faith = kiratism

	color = { 128 46 87 }
	icon = kiratism

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The dead return along the road of the ancestors, and the clan names its forefathers back to the first
		tenet_mountain_worship # Mukkumlung and Sewalungma, the holy mountains of the Yakthung
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # Chasok Tangnam, the offering of the first harvest
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic

		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted # No marriage within the clan, nor within three generations on the mother's side

		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # The phedangma, samba and yeba are men; the yema are women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # A shaman is chosen by the spirits and taught by an older master

rrmeaism = { # Rrmea — the religion of the Qiang: Mubyasei, god of the sky, whose emblem is the white stone set on every roof; the mountain gods, the gods of the house, the hearth and the fields; the xbi, who wear the monkey-skin hat and beat the sheepskin drum, heal the sick and drive out Dul; the sheep sacrificed to the mountain at the New Year; the dead burned on the pyre. Main rite
	name = rrmea
	desc = rrmea_desc
	faith = rrmeaism

	color = { 0.75 0.74 0.84 }
	icon = rrmeaism

	tenets = {
		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # The white stones set on every roof and shrine, and the sacred grove of each village
		tenet_mountain_worship # The mountain gods, to whom each village climbs to sacrifice a sheep
		tenet_ancestor_worship
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_polygamy # A son took his father's widows and a man his brother's widow, so that no woman was left without a husband (Hou Hanshu)
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted # Kin married only after twelve generations had passed (Hou Hanshu)
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The xbi heal the sick, drive out the dul and send the dead on their way
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The xbi are men, each taught by a master
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime # He who killed a man paid with his life; there was no other law (Hou Hanshu)
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_crime # Witch-women, said to take the shape of cats and poison their neighbours, are hunted out by the xbi
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_cremation # The Di and Qiang feared only that their bodies would not be burned after death (Lüshi Chunqiu)
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The chants of the xbi, recited from memory; the Qiang had no script
		mz_doctrine_sinitic_syncretism # The loose-rein prefectures of the Tang and Song among the Qiang of the Min valley
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Grain wine sucked through bamboo straws from a shared jar at every rite
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

	localization = {
		HighGodName = rrmea_high_god_name
		HighGodName2 = rrmea_high_god_name
		HighGodNamePossessive = rrmea_high_god_name_possessive

utaki = { # Okinawan — the religion of Okinawa and Amami: Amamichu, who came down from heaven and made the islands and their sacred groves; Nirai Kanai, the land beyond the sea from which the gods come each year and to which the dead return; the utaki, groves where the gods descend and no man may enter; the noro, the women who serve the gods for each village, and the yuta who speak for the dead; the spiritual power of a sister over her brother; Hinukan, the god of the hearth. Main rite
	name = uchinaa
	desc = uchinaa_desc
	faith = utaki

	color = { 141 96 255 }
	icon = utaki

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The ancestors in the family tomb, honoured by the whole lineage
		tenet_adorcism # The yuta, women possessed by the gods, who speak for the dead
		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # The utaki, sacred groves no man may enter, where the gods come down from Nirai Kanai
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated # The aji and their men ruled; the sisters kept the gods
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal

		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The noro pray for the harvest and the safety of the village and its boats
		doctrine_clerical_gender_female_only # Only women serve the gods: the noro of each village and the yuta who speak with the dead
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # The noro's office passes to a woman of her line, chosen by the gods
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_stoic # The dead are laid in caves and cliff tombs; when the flesh is gone the women wash the bones and place them in urns
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The omoro, the sacred songs of the noro
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Miki, the sacred rice drink brewed by the noro

sarnaism = { # Singbonga — the religion of the Munda, Ho and Bhumij of Chota Nagpur: Singbonga, the sun, and Chando, the moon; the sarna grove of every village and its pahan; the burial stones of the clans; Sarhul at the blossoming of the sal. Main rite

		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # The sarna, the grove of sal trees left standing when the village was cleared, where the gods of the land live

badava = { # Badava — the religion of the Bhil: Bhagwan above all, Kalika the earth mother, Wagh Deo the tiger, Nandervo of the crops; the badava who speak for the gods in trance; clay horses offered at the hill shrines; carved posts for the dead
	faith = badava

	color = { 140 90 60 }
	icon = custom_faith_horse_02

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The dead are fed at every feast, and carved posts and hero stones raised for them
		tenet_adorcism # The badava, through whom the gods speak in trance
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # Holi, the feast of Kalika after the harvest
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted # No marriage within the clan

		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_crime # The kajalio badava finds the witch and breaks her power
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The dead are buried, fed on the third and twelfth days, and remembered with carved posts and stones
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent

		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The Bharath, the Bhil Mahabharata, sung through the night

manichean_babylon = { # Mihriyya — the followers of the archegos Mihr, who under the Umayyads loosened the rules governing dealings between Manichaeans and outsiders, the wisallat; the larger party of the church of Babylon. Main rite
	name = mihriyya
	desc = mihriyya_desc
	faith = manichean_babylon

	color = { 50 168 82 }
	icon = wizrobed_manichean

	tenets = {
		tenet_gnosticism # The two principles of Light and Darkness, and the light held captive in matter that the knowledge given by Mani sets free
		tenet_asceticism # The seals of the mouth, the hand and the breast that bound the Elect
		tenet_adaptive # Mihr loosened the rules on dealings with outsiders, so that Manichaeans could serve and accept gifts from the officials of the caliph
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_spiritual_head # The archegos, successor of Mani, whose seat was Babylon
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic # Mani held that Zoroaster, the Buddha and Jesus had all been true apostles before him
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy # A Hearer was to be content with one wife
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The Hearers fed the Elect, and the Elect prayed for the Hearers and freed the light in their food
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The teachers, bishops and presbyters were men; women could be Elect, but not of the hierarchy
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_disallowed # The Elect were bound to chastity
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # The archegos named the teachers and bishops
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime # Among the ten commandments of the Hearers
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_any_dynasty_member_crime # The Elect might not kill even an animal, and the Hearers might not kill a man
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_crime # Sorcery was forbidden to the Hearers
		doctrine_pilgrimage_forbidden # No place of pilgrimage; the Bema was kept in every community before the empty seat of Mani
		doctrine_funeral_stoic # The body is a prison of darkness; the soul alone ascends
		doctrine_monasticism_accepted
		doctrine_sacraments_absent # No baptism and no eucharist; the sacred meal of the Elect and the confession of the Hearers took their place
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_scriptural # The Living Gospel, the Treasure of Life, the Pragmateia and the other books written by Mani himself
		mz_doctrine_christian_syncretism # Mani called himself the apostle of Jesus Christ and the Paraclete he had promised
		doctrine_me_intoxication_shunned # The Elect drank no wine
		doctrine_me_ablution_none # Mani forbade the washings of the Baptists among whom he grew up, for water too holds the light
	}
